/**
 * surface-climate-1: one shared, deterministic ANNUAL climate field per body.
 *
 * This is a heuristic-v1 climate atlas (docs/research-planet-biome-generation-
 * 2026-09-14.md "Planet and climate priors"), not a circulation model. It turns
 * catalogue facts (temperatureK, water, type, mass, radius, axial tilt) into a
 * per-direction annual-mean temperature, seasonal half-range, snow, treeline,
 * aridity and forest suitability, so the classifier, tile colour, ground shader,
 * forest presence and tree-species binding (Wave 1/2 of
 * docs/biome-climate-increment-1-plan-2026-10-02.json) all read ONE field
 * instead of three unsynchronised copies of an ad-hoc rule. Model, calibration
 * table and owner amendments: docs/surface-climate-v1.md.
 *
 * Import rules (enforced by tests/surface-climate.test.mjs): only catalog.ts,
 * stellar-evolution.ts, det-math.ts and planet-geophysics.ts (itself catalog.ts
 * only), so rock modules may import this later.
 *
 * surface-climate-1.1-geo-g (relief round 2026-10-03, P4-RELIEF E, owner
 * decision 3): the gravity that sets a catalogue body's lapse rate is
 * planetGeophysics(body).surfaceGravityMS2 (provenance 'geophysics') instead of
 * G*massKg/R^2. CLIMATE_CALIBRATION is unchanged (its sha is unchanged).
 *
 * Determinism: the per-body tables are built only from + - * /, sqrt, min, max,
 * floor, abs and the det-math sine/detAcos (docs/time-and-coordinates-contract.md
 * §1.2 rule 3), so they are bit-identical on every conformant engine. The
 * per-sample path does no allocation and no hash64.
 */
import {G,hash64,noise,type Body,type V3} from './catalog.ts';
import {spinStateFor} from './stellar-evolution.ts';
import {detAcos,detExp,sine} from './det-math.ts';
import {planetGeophysics} from './planet-geophysics.ts';

export const CLIMATE_VERSION='surface-climate-2';

/**
 * Heuristic-v1 constants. A test pins sha256(JSON.stringify(CLIMATE_CALIBRATION)),
 * so changing any value forces a CLIMATE_VERSION bump.
 *
 * Owner amendment 1 (2026-10-02): there is NO single global lapse rate. The plan's
 * LAPSE_K_PER_M is replaced by a per-planet value from surface gravity,
 *   Gamma = LAPSE_MOIST_FACTOR * g / CP_J_PER_KG_K,
 * i.e. the dry adiabatic lapse rate g/cp scaled by a constant moist factor chosen
 * so that Earth (g = 9.81 m/s^2) gets 0.6652*9.81/1004 = 6.4996 K/km, the ICAO
 * standard-atmosphere 6.5 K/km. The result is clamped to
 * [LAPSE_MIN_K_PER_M, LAPSE_MAX_K_PER_M] (1 .. 9.8 K/km; 9.8 K/km is Earth's dry
 * adiabat).
 *
 * Gravity source. surface-climate-1 used g = G*massKg/radiusM^2 from the
 * catalogue (fixed 4800 kg/m^3, so g was proportional to radius: 2.7 .. 12.1
 * m/s^2, Gamma 1.8 .. 8.0 K/km, never clamped). Since surface-climate-1.1-geo-g
 * g = planetGeophysics(body).surfaceGravityMS2 (planet-geophysics-1: per-kind
 * density, so g is no longer monotone in radius). Over the 7622 rocky planets
 * of the first 2000 G001 systems: 2.10 .. 21.3 m/s^2, Gamma 1.39 K/km .. the
 * 9.8 K/km clamp, which 1001 of them (13.1%) reach.
 *
 * OBLIQUITY_QUANTUM_RAD (2^-30 rad, about 1e-9 rad) snaps the folded obliquity to
 * a power-of-two grid so tilt t and its retrograde twin pi-t produce bit-identical
 * tables even though pi-(pi-t) is not always t in floating point.
 */
export const CLIMATE_CALIBRATION=Object.freeze({
  K_LAT_K:60,
  LAPSE_MOIST_FACTOR:0.6652, CP_J_PER_KG_K:1004, LAPSE_MIN_K_PER_M:0.001, LAPSE_MAX_K_PER_M:0.0098,
  DATUM_M:0,
  PDD_CRIT:250, PDD_RAMP:100, YEAR_D:365.25,
  WARM_COL_REF_K:288, WARM_COL_PER_K:0.005, WARM_COL_MIN:0.8, WARM_COL_MAX:1.1,
  TW_CEILING_K:320, TW_CEILING_SCALE_K:40,
  HEAT_LO_K:305, HEAT_HI_K:316, HEAT_ANNUAL_MAX_K:320,
  T_TREE_WARMEST_K:283, TREE_RAMP_K:6,
  SEASON_K:40, MARITIME:0.5,
  ZONAL_A1:1.6, ZONAL_A2:0, ZONAL_A3:0.75, ZONAL_A4:0.4,
  ZONAL_W1:10, ZONAL_W2:12, ZONAL_W3:12, ZONAL_W4:12,
  ZONAL_C2:50, ZONAL_C3:28, ZONAL_C4:88, HADLEY_EDGE_DEG:30,
  PET_MIN_MM:120, PET_MM_PER_BIOTEMP_C:58.93,
  P_REF_MM:650, P_WATER_REF:0.7, P_WATER_MIN:0.2, P_WATER_MAX:1.2, P_TEMP_REF_K:288, P_TEMP_PER_K:0.025, P_TEMP_MIN:0.2,
  P_NOISE_BASE:0.4, P_NOISE_GAIN:1.2, AI_HALF:0.6,
  FOREST_MOIST_LO:0.44, FOREST_MOIST_RAMP:0.08,
  GROW_K:278,
  OBLIQUITY_QUANTUM_RAD:1/1073741824,
});
const C=CLIMATE_CALIBRATION;

/** Latitude rows (x = sin latitude, k/TABLE_ROWS) and orbit samples per table. */
export const TABLE_ROWS=64, ORBIT_SAMPLES=256;

export type ClimateProvenance=Readonly<{
  anchorTempK:'catalogue'|'override'; obliquityRad:'catalogue'|'override';
  surfaceGravityMS2:'catalogue-derived'|'geophysics'|'override'; lapseKPerM:'derived-heuristic'|'override';
  table:'derived-heuristic';
}>;
export type ClimateForcing=Readonly<{
  version:typeof CLIMATE_VERSION;
  anchorTempK:number;
  /** Effective obliquity folded to [0, pi/2] and quantised (see OBLIQUITY_QUANTUM_RAD). */
  obliquityRad:number;
  water:number; frozen:boolean; habitat:boolean; moistureSeed:number;
  surfaceGravityMS2:number; lapseKPerM:number;
  /** Annual-mean daily insolation per row, normalised so its area mean is 1. Do not mutate. */
  qMean:Float64Array;
  /** Seasonal half-range (max-min)/2 of daily insolation per row, same normalisation. Do not mutate. */
  qAmp:Float64Array;
  /** surface-climate-2: sin(latitude) of the ITCZ = the argmax row of qMean (equator below ~54 deg
   * obliquity, the poles above), its latitude (deg) and the fixed Hadley edge (owner Q4: 30 deg). */
  itczSinLat:number; itczLatDeg:number; hadleyEdgeDeg:number;
  /** Zonal precipitation factor Z and the subsidence diagnostic (dryBelt01) per x = sin|lat|,
   * ZONAL_ROWS+1 rows. Do not mutate. */
  zonal:Float64Array; subsidence:Float64Array;
  provenance:ClimateProvenance;
}>;

export type ClimateSample={
  /** Annual-mean daily insolation relative to the planet's area mean (1 = global mean). */
  insolation01:number;
  /** Seasonal half-range of daily insolation, same units as insolation01. */
  insolationHalfRange:number;
  annualMeanTempK:number;
  seasonalHalfRangeK:number;
  warmestMonthK:number;
  coldestMonthK:number;
  legacyMoisture:number;
  dryBelt01:number;
  effectiveMoisture:number;
  aridity01:number;
  /** Positive degree-days above 0 C (C*d per year) of the sinusoidal year (surface-climate-2). */
  pdd:number;
  /** Annual precipitation index (mm/yr, surface-climate-2 heuristic; the climate grid multiplies it in M2). */
  precipMm:number;
  /** Potential evapotranspiration (mm/yr, Holdridge biotemperature x 58.93, floored at PET_MIN_MM). */
  petMm:number;
  snowFraction:number;
  treeline01:number;
  growingSeason01:number;
  forestSuitability01:number;
  climateCode:number;
};
export function createClimateSample():ClimateSample {
  return {insolation01:0,insolationHalfRange:0,annualMeanTempK:0,seasonalHalfRangeK:0,warmestMonthK:0,coldestMonthK:0,
    legacyMoisture:0,dryBelt01:0,effectiveMoisture:0,aridity01:0,pdd:0,precipMm:0,petMm:0,snowFraction:0,treeline01:0,growingSeason01:0,
    forestSuitability01:0,climateCode:0};
}

const clamp01=(v:number)=>v<0?0:v>1?1:v;
/** Arithmetic smoothstep (no platform transcendental). */
const smooth=(x:number,lo:number,hi:number)=>{const t=clamp01((x-lo)/(hi-lo));return t*t*(3-2*t);};

/**
 * surface-climate-2 positive-degree-day kernel. The year is the sinusoid
 * T(t) = mu + A cos(2 pi t) (mu = annual mean, A = seasonal half-range). With
 * v = (mu - thr)/A, the time-mean of max(0, T - thr) is A*H(v) and the
 * fraction of the year with T > thr is G(v), where
 *   H(v) = (sqrt(1-v^2) + v*acos(-v))/pi,   G(v) = acos(-v)/pi,
 * H(v) = 0 and G(v) = 0 for v <= -1, H(v) = v and G(v) = 1 for v >= 1 (both
 * continuous at +-1). Both are tabulated ONCE at module init on 513 nodes over
 * v in [-1,1] (detAcos only here) and read with linear interpolation, so the
 * per-sample path has no transcendental at all.
 */
export const KERNEL_STEPS=512;
const H_TAB=new Float64Array(KERNEL_STEPS+1),G_TAB=new Float64Array(KERNEL_STEPS+1);
for(let k=0;k<=KERNEL_STEPS;k++){
  const v=-1+2*k/KERNEL_STEPS,a=detAcos(-v);
  H_TAB[k]=(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-v*v))+v*a)/Math.PI;G_TAB[k]=a/Math.PI;
}
// Exact end nodes (H(-1) = G(-1) = 0, H(1) = G(1) = 1), whatever detAcos rounds to.
H_TAB[0]=0;G_TAB[0]=0;H_TAB[KERNEL_STEPS]=1;G_TAB[KERNEL_STEPS]=1;
/** Linear interpolation in a kernel table for v strictly inside (-1, 1). */
function kernelAt(tab:Float64Array,v:number):number {
  const f=(v+1)*(KERNEL_STEPS/2);
  let i=Math.floor(f);if(i<0)i=0;else if(i>KERNEL_STEPS-1)i=KERNEL_STEPS-1;
  return tab[i]!+(tab[i+1]!-tab[i]!)*(f-i);
}
/** The tabulated H(v) (mean positive excess per unit half-range). */
export function pddKernelH(v:number):number {return v<=-1?0:v>=1?v:kernelAt(H_TAB,v);}
/** The tabulated G(v) (fraction of the year above the threshold). */
export function pddKernelG(v:number):number {return v<=-1?0:v>=1?1:kernelAt(G_TAB,v);}
/**
 * Degree-days (C*d per year) above `thresholdC` of a sinusoidal year with annual
 * mean `annualK` (K) and half-range `halfK` (K). A < 1e-9 is a seasonless year.
 */
export function degreeDaysAbove(annualK:number,halfK:number,thresholdC:number):number {
  const excess=annualK-273.15-thresholdC;
  if(halfK<1e-9)return excess>0?excess*C.YEAR_D:0;
  return C.YEAR_D*halfK*pddKernelH(excess/halfK);
}

/**
 * Byte-identical to the renderer's habitat gate (planet-renderer.ts:830) and
 * equivalent to surface-biomes.ts's forestPotential body clause for every
 * non-Ice body (Ice worlds have snow=1, so their forest is false either way).
 */
export function temperateHabitat(body:Body):boolean {
  return body.water>0&&body.temperatureK>=250&&body.temperatureK<=325&&body.type!=='Ice world'&&body.type!=='Volcanic world';
}

/**
 * surface-climate-2 step 2, warm-column lapse correction (a calibration choice,
 * docs/surface-climate-v2.md): the lapse rate applied above the datum is
 * lapse*f(T0), f = clamp(1 - WARM_COL_PER_K*(T0 - WARM_COL_REF_K), 0.8, 1.1),
 * T0 the datum annual mean of the sample's latitude. Warm (moist, tropical)
 * columns cool more slowly with height than the planet's mean lapse rate, cold
 * columns faster. Without it the Earth equatorial snow line is 4302 m, below
 * the 4450 m floor.
 */
export function warmColumnLapseFactor(datumAnnualK:number):number {
  const f=1-C.WARM_COL_PER_K*(datumAnnualK-C.WARM_COL_REF_K);
  return f<C.WARM_COL_MIN?C.WARM_COL_MIN:f>C.WARM_COL_MAX?C.WARM_COL_MAX:f;
}

/**
 * surface-climate-2 step 3, warmest-month ceiling (symmetric): above
 * TW_CEILING_K the raw warmest month TwRaw = annual + half saturates,
 *   Tw' = 320 + e/(1 + e/40), e = TwRaw - 320   (Tw' < 360 K always),
 * and the half-range becomes half' = Tw' - annual, so the coldest month
 * annual - half' rises by the same amount and the annual mean is unchanged.
 * half' is never negative (the warmest month is never below the annual mean);
 * that floor binds only where the annual mean itself exceeds the saturated
 * warmest month (a hot, nearly seasonless column; not a habitat world).
 * PDD, treeline, growing season and the tile colour all read half'.
 */
export function warmestMonthCeilingK(rawWarmestK:number):number {
  const e=rawWarmestK-C.TW_CEILING_K;
  return e>0?C.TW_CEILING_K+e/(1+e/C.TW_CEILING_SCALE_K):rawWarmestK;
}

/** Surface gravity from the catalogue body, g = G*M/R^2 (m/s^2). */
export function surfaceGravityMS2(body:Pick<Body,'massKg'|'radiusM'>):number {
  return G*body.massKg/(body.radiusM*body.radiusM);
}
/** Per-planet environmental lapse rate (K/m) from surface gravity; see CLIMATE_CALIBRATION. */
export function lapseRateForGravity(gMS2:number):number {
  const raw=C.LAPSE_MOIST_FACTOR*gMS2/C.CP_J_PER_KG_K;
  return raw<C.LAPSE_MIN_K_PER_M?C.LAPSE_MIN_K_PER_M:raw>C.LAPSE_MAX_K_PER_M?C.LAPSE_MAX_K_PER_M:raw;
}

/**
 * Fold a drawn axial tilt in [0, pi] (retrograde included, stellar-evolution.ts
 * spinStateFor) to the insolation-relevant obliquity in [0, pi/2]: a spin axis
 * tilted by t and one tilted by pi-t sweep the same declination cycle, only the
 * sense of rotation differs. Snapped to OBLIQUITY_QUANTUM_RAD.
 */
export function effectiveObliquityRad(axialTiltRad:number):number {
  const t=axialTiltRad<0?0:axialTiltRad>Math.PI?Math.PI:axialTiltRad;
  const folded=t<Math.PI-t?t:Math.PI-t;
  return Math.floor(folded/C.OBLIQUITY_QUANTUM_RAD+0.5)*C.OBLIQUITY_QUANTUM_RAD;
}

// sin(lambda) of the 256 mid-point true longitudes, shared by every table.
const SIN_LAMBDA=new Float64Array(ORBIT_SAMPLES);
for(let j=0;j<ORBIT_SAMPLES;j++)SIN_LAMBDA[j]=sine((j+.5)*2*Math.PI/ORBIT_SAMPLES);

/**
 * Annual-mean and seasonal half-range of DAILY-MEAN top-of-atmosphere insolation
 * versus x = sin(latitude), for one obliquity, in units of the area mean.
 *
 * Daily mean (Berger 1978, "Long-term variations of daily insolation and
 * Quaternary climatic changes", J. Atmos. Sci. 35:2362, eq. for W_d; also
 * Hartmann, Global Physical Climatology, eq. 2.15):
 *   Q = (S0/pi) (a/r)^2 [h0 sin(phi) sin(delta) + cos(phi) cos(delta) sin(h0)],
 *   cos(h0) = -tan(phi) tan(delta), clamped to [-1,1] (h0 = 0 polar night,
 *   h0 = pi polar day), and sin(delta) = sin(eps) sin(lambda) for true longitude
 *   lambda. Eccentricity is ignored: (a/r)^2 averages to a uniform factor
 *   1/sqrt(1-e^2) in the annual mean, which the area normalisation removes; it
 *   only skews seasons, which this annual model does not resolve. With e = 0,
 *   lambda advances uniformly in time, so the 256 mid-point samples are an
 *   equal-time annual average.
 * Northern and southern rows are identical with e = 0 (the cycle is only shifted
 * by half a year), so one table over |x| serves both hemispheres. The result is
 * correct for any obliquity: above ~54 deg the poles receive more annual
 * insolation than the equator (annual polar mean is S0 sin(eps)/pi).
 */
export function insolationTables(obliquityRad:number):{qMean:Float64Array;qAmp:Float64Array} {
  const qMean=new Float64Array(TABLE_ROWS+1),qAmp=new Float64Array(TABLE_ROWS+1);
  const sinEps=sine(obliquityRad);
  for(let k=0;k<=TABLE_ROWS;k++){
    const x=k/TABLE_ROWS,cosPhi=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-x*x));
    let sum=0,max=-1,min=2;
    for(let j=0;j<ORBIT_SAMPLES;j++){
      const sinDelta=sinEps*SIN_LAMBDA[j]!,cosDelta=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-sinDelta*sinDelta));
      const denom=cosPhi*cosDelta;
      let q:number;
      // Pole (cosPhi=0) or sun overhead at the pole (cosDelta=0): the sun's
      // elevation is constant over the day, so Q = max(0, sin(phi) sin(delta)).
      if(denom<1e-12)q=Math.max(0,x*sinDelta);
      else{
        const c=-(x*sinDelta)/denom;
        // Polar night (c>=1, h0=0) and polar day (c<=-1, h0=pi) short-cut the
        // same formula exactly: detAcos(1)=0 and detAcos(-1)=pi bit-for-bit.
        if(c>=1)q=0;
        else if(c<=-1)q=x*sinDelta;
        else{
          const h0=detAcos(c);
          q=(h0*x*sinDelta+denom*Math.sqrt(1-c*c))/Math.PI;
        }
      }
      sum+=q;if(q>max)max=q;if(q<min)min=q;
    }
    qMean[k]=sum/ORBIT_SAMPLES;qAmp[k]=(max-min)/2;
  }
  // dA is proportional to d(sin lat), so the area mean over a hemisphere is a
  // trapezoid in x. Analytically this is 1/4 (global mean = S0/4).
  let area=0;
  for(let k=0;k<TABLE_ROWS;k++)area+=(qMean[k]!+qMean[k+1]!)/2;
  area/=TABLE_ROWS;
  for(let k=0;k<=TABLE_ROWS;k++){qMean[k]=qMean[k]!/area;qAmp[k]=qAmp[k]!/area;}
  return {qMean,qAmp};
}

export type ClimateForcingParams={
  anchorTempK:number; obliquityRad:number; water:number;
  frozen?:boolean; habitat?:boolean; moistureSeed?:number;
  surfaceGravityMS2?:number; lapseKPerM?:number;
  provenance?:Partial<ClimateProvenance>;
};
/**
 * Build a forcing from explicit parameters (synthetic/reference worlds, e.g. the
 * Earth reference check). obliquityRad is folded and quantised exactly like a
 * catalogue tilt. Without lapseKPerM the lapse rate comes from surfaceGravityMS2
 * (default Earth 9.81).
 */
export function climateForcingFromParams(p:ClimateForcingParams):ClimateForcing {
  const obliquityRad=effectiveObliquityRad(p.obliquityRad);
  const g=p.surfaceGravityMS2??9.81;
  const lapseKPerM=p.lapseKPerM??lapseRateForGravity(g);
  const {qMean,qAmp}=insolationTables(obliquityRad);
  const itczSinLat=itczSinLatOf(qMean),itczLatDeg=LAT_DEG[Math.round(itczSinLat*ZONAL_ROWS)]!;
  const {zonal,subsidence}=zonalTables(itczLatDeg);
  return Object.freeze({version:CLIMATE_VERSION,anchorTempK:p.anchorTempK,obliquityRad,water:p.water,
    frozen:!!p.frozen,habitat:p.habitat??true,moistureSeed:p.moistureSeed??0,surfaceGravityMS2:g,lapseKPerM,qMean,qAmp,
    itczSinLat,itczLatDeg,hadleyEdgeDeg:C.HADLEY_EDGE_DEG,zonal,subsidence,
    provenance:Object.freeze({anchorTempK:'override',obliquityRad:'override',surfaceGravityMS2:'override',
      lapseKPerM:p.lapseKPerM===undefined?'derived-heuristic':'override',table:'derived-heuristic',...p.provenance}) as ClimateProvenance});
}

/**
 * surface-climate-2 step 6, the ITCZ zonal band. Rows k = 0..ZONAL_ROWS over
 * x = sin|latitude| = k/ZONAL_ROWS; LAT_DEG is their latitude (detAcos at module
 * init only). Per forcing, with dI = |lat - itczLatDeg| (deg),
 *   Z(dI) = 1 + A1 e^-(dI/W1)^2 + A2 e^-((dI-50)/W2)^2 - A3 e^-((dI-28)/W3)^2 - A4 e^-((dI-88)/W4)^2
 * (ITCZ convergence, mid-latitude storm track, Hadley subsidence, polar desert;
 * the squares are products and e^ is detExp) and the subsidence term
 * e^-((dI-28)/W3)^2 alone is the dryBelt01 diagnostic. Both move with the ITCZ,
 * so on a high-tilt world (ITCZ at the pole) the wet band is polar and the dry
 * band follows it. Calibration: docs/surface-climate-v2.md.
 */
export const ZONAL_ROWS=512;
const LAT_DEG=new Float64Array(ZONAL_ROWS+1);
for(let k=0;k<=ZONAL_ROWS;k++)LAT_DEG[k]=90-detAcos(k/ZONAL_ROWS)*180/Math.PI;
LAT_DEG[0]=0;LAT_DEG[ZONAL_ROWS]=90;
const gauss=(x:number,w:number)=>{const u=x/w;return detExp(-(u*u));};
/** The ITCZ row of a qMean table (first maximum) as sin(latitude). */
export function itczSinLatOf(qMean:Float64Array):number {
  let best=0;for(let k=1;k<=TABLE_ROWS;k++)if(qMean[k]!>qMean[best]!)best=k;
  return best/TABLE_ROWS;
}
/** Zonal factor Z and subsidence diagnostic tables for an ITCZ latitude (deg). */
export function zonalTables(itczLatDeg:number):{zonal:Float64Array;subsidence:Float64Array} {
  const zonal=new Float64Array(ZONAL_ROWS+1),subsidence=new Float64Array(ZONAL_ROWS+1);
  for(let k=0;k<=ZONAL_ROWS;k++){
    const dI=LAT_DEG[k]!>itczLatDeg?LAT_DEG[k]!-itczLatDeg:itczLatDeg-LAT_DEG[k]!;
    const sub=gauss(dI-C.ZONAL_C3,C.ZONAL_W3);
    zonal[k]=1+C.ZONAL_A1*gauss(dI,C.ZONAL_W1)+C.ZONAL_A2*gauss(dI-C.ZONAL_C2,C.ZONAL_W2)-C.ZONAL_A3*sub-C.ZONAL_A4*gauss(dI-C.ZONAL_C4,C.ZONAL_W4);
    subsidence[k]=sub;
  }
  return {zonal,subsidence};
}

const FORCING_CACHE_CAP=64;
const forcingCache=new Map<string,ClimateForcing>();

/**
 * The climate forcing of a catalogue body, cached (FIFO, at most 64 bodies;
 * eviction never changes values because the forcing is a pure function of the
 * key, which holds every body field the forcing reads). opts.anchorTempK replaces body.temperatureK (e.g. a dated or
 * luminosity-corrected temperature later); opts.lapseKPerM overrides the
 * gravity-derived lapse rate for calibration studies only.
 */
export function climateForcingFor(body:Body,opts?:{anchorTempK?:number;lapseKPerM?:number}):ClimateForcing {
  const anchorTempK=opts?.anchorTempK??body.temperatureK;
  // Every body field the forcing reads is in the key (water/type feed frozen and
  // habitat, mass/radius feed gravity), so a derived body that shares a seed and
  // temperature with a catalogue body never reads that body's forcing.
  const key=[body.seed,body.kind,anchorTempK,body.water,body.type,body.massKg,body.radiusM].join('|')+(opts?.lapseKPerM===undefined?'':'|lapse='+opts.lapseKPerM);
  const hit=forcingCache.get(key);
  if(hit)return hit;
  // surface-climate-1.1-geo-g: surface physics gravity (planet-geophysics-1),
  // never the catalogue orbital mass (owner decisions 3 and 4).
  const g=planetGeophysics(body).surfaceGravityMS2;
  const forcing=climateForcingFromParams({
    anchorTempK,obliquityRad:spinStateFor(body).axialTiltRad,water:body.water,
    frozen:body.type==='Ice world',habitat:temperateHabitat(body),
    moistureSeed:Number(hash64(body.seed+'/macro-v1')&0xffffffffn),
    surfaceGravityMS2:g,lapseKPerM:opts?.lapseKPerM,
    provenance:{anchorTempK:opts?.anchorTempK===undefined?'catalogue':'override',obliquityRad:'catalogue',
      surfaceGravityMS2:'geophysics',lapseKPerM:opts?.lapseKPerM===undefined?'derived-heuristic':'override'},
  });
  if(forcingCache.size>=FORCING_CACHE_CAP){const oldest=forcingCache.keys().next().value;if(oldest!==undefined)forcingCache.delete(oldest);}
  forcingCache.set(key,forcing);
  return forcing;
}
/** Test hook: drop every cached forcing (values never depend on cache state). */
export function clearClimateForcingCache():void {forcingCache.clear();}
export function climateForcingCacheSize():number {return forcingCache.size;}

/**
 * Per-direction annual climate sampler. `d` is the unit body-frame direction
 * (spin axis = +Y, as everywhere in the surface code); heightM is the
 * post-landform ground height above the datum. Writes into and returns `out`;
 * no allocation, no hash64 per call.
 */
export function surfaceClimate(forcing:ClimateForcing):(d:V3|readonly [number,number,number],heightM:number,out:ClimateSample)=>ClimateSample {
  const {qMean,qAmp,zonal,subsidence,anchorTempK:anchor,lapseKPerM:lapse,water,frozen,habitat,moistureSeed}=forcing;
  const seed=moistureSeed+30;
  const maritime=1-C.MARITIME*water;
  // Step 5: the water term of the precipitation index (per forcing).
  const wr=water/C.P_WATER_REF,pWater=C.P_REF_MM*(wr<C.P_WATER_MIN?C.P_WATER_MIN:wr>C.P_WATER_MAX?C.P_WATER_MAX:wr);
  return (d,heightM,out)=>{
    let x=d[1]<0?-d[1]:d[1];
    if(x>1)x=1;
    const f=x*TABLE_ROWS;
    let i=Math.floor(f);if(i>TABLE_ROWS-1)i=TABLE_ROWS-1;
    const t=f-i;
    const q=qMean[i]!+(qMean[i+1]!-qMean[i]!)*t;
    const qa=qAmp[i]!+(qAmp[i+1]!-qAmp[i]!)*t;
    const above=heightM-C.DATUM_M;
    const datumAnnual=anchor+C.K_LAT_K*(q-1);
    // Step 2: warm-column lapse (warmColumnLapseFactor, inlined).
    let fac=1-C.WARM_COL_PER_K*(datumAnnual-C.WARM_COL_REF_K);
    if(fac<C.WARM_COL_MIN)fac=C.WARM_COL_MIN;else if(fac>C.WARM_COL_MAX)fac=C.WARM_COL_MAX;
    const annual=datumAnnual-lapse*fac*(above>0?above:0);
    const halfRaw=C.SEASON_K*qa*maritime;
    // Step 3: warmest-month ceiling (warmestMonthCeilingK, inlined), symmetric half-range.
    let half=halfRaw;
    const excess=annual+halfRaw-C.TW_CEILING_K;
    if(excess>0){half=C.TW_CEILING_K+excess/(1+excess/C.TW_CEILING_SCALE_K)-annual;if(half<0)half=0;}
    // Exactly the legacy classifier's moisture expression (surface-biomes.ts).
    const moisture=noise(d[0]*5+13,d[1]*5+37,d[2]*5+19,seed);
    // Step 6: the ITCZ zonal band, one interpolation in the forcing's tables.
    const fz=x*ZONAL_ROWS;
    let iz=Math.floor(fz);if(iz>ZONAL_ROWS-1)iz=ZONAL_ROWS-1;
    const tz=fz-iz;
    const zf=zonal[iz]!+(zonal[iz+1]!-zonal[iz]!)*tz;
    const belt=subsidence[iz]!+(subsidence[iz+1]!-subsidence[iz]!)*tz;
    // surface-climate-2 step 1: snow from positive degree-days (melt), not the
    // annual mean. snowFraction > 0.6 (the 'Alpine / polar snow' label) is PDD < 190.
    const pdd0=degreeDaysAbove(annual,half,0);
    // Step 5: effective moisture from a precipitation / PET aridity index.
    // PET from Holdridge biotemperature (mean of the temperature clipped to 0..30 C):
    // biotemp = (PDD above 0 C - PDD above 30 C)/year.
    const biotemp=(pdd0-degreeDaysAbove(annual,half,30))/C.YEAR_D;
    let pet=C.PET_MM_PER_BIOTEMP_C*biotemp;if(pet<C.PET_MIN_MM)pet=C.PET_MIN_MM;
    let pTemp=1+C.P_TEMP_PER_K*(annual-C.P_TEMP_REF_K);if(pTemp<C.P_TEMP_MIN)pTemp=C.P_TEMP_MIN;
    const precip=pWater*pTemp*zf*(C.P_NOISE_BASE+C.P_NOISE_GAIN*moisture);
    const ai=precip/pet,eff=ai/(ai+C.AI_HALF);
    const snow=frozen?1:water>0?clamp01((C.PDD_CRIT-pdd0)/C.PDD_RAMP):0;
    const warmest=annual+half;
    const treeline=clamp01((warmest-C.T_TREE_WARMEST_K)/C.TREE_RAMP_K);
    // Fraction of the year above GROW_K: G((annual - GROW_K)/half), tabulated.
    const grow=half<=1e-9?(annual>C.GROW_K?1:0):pddKernelG((annual-C.GROW_K)/half);
    let tBin=Math.floor((annual-anchor+32)/2);if(tBin<0)tBin=0;else if(tBin>31)tBin=31;
    let mBin=Math.floor(eff*8);if(mBin<0)mBin=0;else if(mBin>7)mBin=7;
    out.insolation01=q;out.insolationHalfRange=qa;
    out.annualMeanTempK=annual;out.seasonalHalfRangeK=half;out.warmestMonthK=warmest;out.coldestMonthK=annual-half;
    out.legacyMoisture=moisture;out.dryBelt01=belt;out.effectiveMoisture=eff;out.precipMm=precip;out.petMm=pet;
    out.aridity01=clamp01((0.5-eff)*4);
    out.pdd=pdd0;out.snowFraction=snow;out.treeline01=treeline;out.growingSeason01=grow;
    // Step 4: heat cap. Forest fades out between a 305 K and a 316 K warmest month
    // and is 0 wherever the annual mean reaches 320 K.
    const heat=annual>=C.HEAT_ANNUAL_MAX_K?0:1-smooth(warmest,C.HEAT_LO_K,C.HEAT_HI_K);
    out.forestSuitability01=habitat?treeline*clamp01((eff-C.FOREST_MOIST_LO)/C.FOREST_MOIST_RAMP)*clamp01(1-snow/0.2)*heat:0;
    out.climateCode=(tBin<<3)|mBin;
    return out;
  };
}

/** Bin centres of a climate code: temperature relative to the anchor, and effective moisture. */
export function decodeClimateCode(code:number):{deltaTempK:number;moisture:number} {
  const tBin=(code>>3)&31,mBin=code&7;
  return {deltaTempK:tBin*2-32+1,moisture:(mBin+0.5)/8};
}

/**
 * Datum height (m) above which the annual-mean temperature at sin-latitude x is
 * below `thresholdK`. Negative means the datum is already colder. Pure helper for
 * reports and tests. Since surface-climate-2 snow is NOT an annual-mean isotherm
 * (it follows positive degree-days, see degreeDaysAbove).
 */
export function isothermHeightM(forcing:ClimateForcing,x:number,thresholdK:number):number {
  const out=createClimateSample();
  surfaceClimate(forcing)([0,x,0],C.DATUM_M,out);
  // surface-climate-2: the column's effective lapse includes the warm-column factor.
  return C.DATUM_M+(out.annualMeanTempK-thresholdK)/(forcing.lapseKPerM*warmColumnLapseFactor(out.annualMeanTempK));
}

# surface-climate-2 (BIOME2-CLIMATE, milestone M1)

Package R5-ATLAS-CLIMATE-M1, branch `climate/m1`. Plan: `docs/biome-climate-increment-2-plan-v2-2026-10-04.json`
(P1, M1 steps 1-7; v1 formulas). Versions: `CLIMATE_VERSION` `surface-climate-1.1-geo-g` -> `surface-climate-2`;
`TREE_HABITAT_VERSION` `tree-habitat-2-climate` -> `tree-habitat-3-heat` (expression unchanged; the heat cap moves the
`forestSuitability01` it reads). `TERRAIN_VERSION` is not touched here (coordinator).

**Status: STOP at step 8.** The harness site SITE_A fails the complete SITE_RULE after M1, and the existing
deterministic search finds no replacement in its 100 km disc (section 6). Steps 1-7 are implemented and pass their
own acceptance. Nothing was retuned to keep the site.

## 1. Model (all per sample, no per-sample transcendental)

| Step | Change | Formula |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | PDD snow | Sinusoidal year, `PDD(T0) = 365.25 * half * H((annual - T0)/half)`, `H(v) = (sqrt(1-v^2) + v acos(-v))/pi`; `snowFraction = clamp01((250 - PDD0)/100)`. H and G (`acos(-v)/pi`, the fraction of the year above a threshold) are tabulated on 513 nodes at module init with `detAcos` and read by linear interpolation. The label 'Alpine / polar snow' (snowFraction > 0.6) means PDD0 < 190 C d; ELA tests use the label. |
| 2 | Warm-column lapse | `lapse * clamp(1 - 0.005 (T_datum - 288), 0.8, 1.1)`. Mandatory: without it the Earth equator label ELA is 4302 m (< 4450). |
| 3 | Warmest-month ceiling | `Tw' = 320 + e/(1 + e/40)` for the excess e above 320 K; the half-range is rebuilt symmetrically from Tw' and floored at 0, the annual mean is unchanged. |
| 4 | Heat cap | `forest *= 1 - smooth(Tw', 305, 316)`; forest is 0 at annual >= 320 K. |
| 5 | PET effective moisture | `PET = max(120, 58.93 * biotemp)` (Holdridge), `biotemp = (PDD0 - PDD30)/365.25`; `P = P_REF * clamp(water/0.7, 0.2, 1.2) * max(0.2, 1 + 0.025 (annual - 288)) * Z * (0.4 + 1.2 noise)`; `effM = AI/(AI + 0.6)`, `AI = P/PET`. The 0.6 half-point keeps the classifier thresholds at their aridity-index meaning. |
| 6 | ITCZ zonal band | `Z(dI) = 1 + 1.6 g(dI,10) + 0 g(dI-50,12) - 0.75 g(dI-28,12) - 0.4 g(dI-88,12)`, `g(x,w) = exp(-(x/w)^2)`, `dI = abs(lat - ITCZ)`. ITCZ = argmax row of the annual insolation table (equator below ~54 deg obliquity, a pole above). Z and the subsidence term are built per forcing as 513-row tables over x = sin abs(lat) with `detExp`; a sample does one interpolation. `dryBelt01` is now the subsidence term. Hadley edge fixed at 30 deg (Q4). |

New `ClimateSample` fields: `pdd` (C d above 0 C), `precipMm`, `petMm`. Removed calibration: `T_SNOW_K`, `SNOW_RAMP_K`,
`BELT_LO/HI/EDGE`, `BELT_MOISTURE`. New forcing fields: `itczSinLat`, `itczLatDeg`, `hadleyEdgeDeg`, `zonal`, `subsidence`.

## 2. Decisions

All are Opus-decided on Kasper's instruction and can be overruled.

- **Q2 -> (a).** Move the harness site using the existing deterministic search after M1. Add "centre and rings in
  {4,5,6} with >= 1 K Tc margin" to SITE_RULE. REFERENCE e6a0b39 stays unchanged. Result: STOP (section 6).
- **Q4.** The wind belts stay at 30 deg from the ITCZ (`hadleyEdgeDeg` 30). The subsidence centre is 28 deg.
- **Q6.** The heat cap ships as physics. Consequence, stated plainly: **Selawyn and Raiva lose almost all of their good
  forest.** Selawyn falls from 48.7 % of land to 2.0 %, and Raiva from 47.8 % to 0.2 %. Across the corpus, the median
  share of land with forest > 0.5 falls from 42.1 % to 4.7 %.
- **Half-range floor.** After the ceiling, the rebuilt half-range is floored at 0.
- **P_REF_MM 1000 -> 650 (new, step 6).** With P_REF 1000, none of the 7776 round-1 candidates and none of the 15840
  round-2 coarse candidates met the plan's Earth targets. The cause is that mid-latitude effM sits near 0.8, and the
  required 1.2x P(45-60)/P(15-35) ratio rules out a mid-latitude dip. P_REF is the one number that sets P against PET,
  so it was calibrated together with the band.

## 3. Calibration (step 6)

The calibration uses only the Earth reference: 288 K, 23.44 deg, water 0.7, g 9.81, all directions, h = 0, noise
included. It reads no catalogue planet, no SITE_A value and no corpus value.

Targets (plan v1 P1 step 6), each needing a margin of at least 0.02:

- P(15-35) <= 0.55 P(0-10)
- P(45-60) >= 1.2 P(15-35)
- P(>75) < 0.35 P(0-10)
- effM(0-10) >= 0.6
- effM(18-32) <= 0.40
- effM(45-60) in 0.45-0.65

Choice rule: among the passing candidates, take the one closest to the plan's starting band.

The scans are in `test-results/climate-m1/calibrate-zonal*.mjs` and `*.out.jsonl`:

| Round | Candidates scanned | Passing |
|---|---|---|
| Round 1 | 7776 | 0 |
| Round 2, coarse | 15840 | 0 (best margin 0.006) |
| Round 2, fine | 12960 | 240 |

The chosen candidate is [a 1.6, 0, 0.75, 0.4; w 10, 12, 12, 12; PET_MIN 120; P_REF 650], with distance 2.5 and
minimum margin 0.021. Its live values on the Earth reference:

| Quantity | Value | Target |
|---|---|---|
| P(15-35)/P(0-10) | 0.199 | <= 0.55 |
| P(45-60)/P(15-35) | 1.221 | >= 1.2 |
| P(>75)/P(0-10) | 0.072 | < 0.35 |
| effM(0-10) | 0.627 | >= 0.6 |
| effM(18-32) | 0.264 | <= 0.40 |
| effM(45-60) | 0.627 | 0.45-0.65 |

The binding constraint is P(45-60)/P(15-35) (margin 0.021). More robust candidates exist, such as
[2, 0, 0.85, 0.4; 12, 12, 16, 12; 120; 625] with margin 0.05. They were not chosen because the choice rule picks the
closest candidate, not the most robust one.

Negative tests, each kept in the suite:

- The shipped annual-mean snow rule gives a 60 deg label ELA of 810 m, which fails the 1200-1700 m band
  (`tests/climate-earth-reference.test.mjs`).
- The shipped fixed dry belt fails the polar-ITCZ test on 93 of the 213 judged planets
  (`tests/acceptance/climate-agreement.test.mjs`).
- Without the warm-column factor, the equator label ELA is 4302 m.
- The uncalibrated starting band misses the Earth targets: effM(18-32) is 0.472 and effM(45-60) is 0.799.

## 5. Re-baseline against the prototype (plan v2 expectedOutcomes)

| Quantity | Shipped | Prototype M1 (uncalibrated) | After M1 (calibrated) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Maithea snow label | 19.7 % | about 20 % | 20.5 % |
| Maithea range snow (> 3 km, abs(lat) < 60) | 27.5 % | about 36 % | 34.4 % |
| Lethuris snow label | 20.9 % | about 11 % | 11.0 % |
| Selawyn max Tw' | 388.8 K | about 345 K | 345.3 K |
| Selawyn forest > 0.5 | 48.7 % | 2-4 % | 2.0 % |
| Raiva forest > 0.5 | 47.8 % | 6-10 % | 0.2 % |
| Corpus snow label median | 6.8 % | about 3 % | 3.5 % |
| Corpus forest > 0.5 median | 42.1 % | about 12 % | 4.7 % |
| Maithea effM p10 / p50 / p90 | .26 / .484 / .715 | .599 median | .196 / .488 / .672 |

Two numbers come out below the prototype: Raiva forest (0.2 % against 6-10 %) and corpus forest (4.7 % against about
12 %). The prototype's moisture was uncalibrated and wetter. The calibrated subtropics are now dry (Earth effM(18-32) =
0.26), so less forest survives the heat cap. Raiva's ITCZ is polar: its tropics lie 60-90 deg from the ITCZ and receive
the polar-desert term (effM 0.34-0.44 at 0-15 deg).

## 6. SITE_A (step 8): STOP

SITE_A (`tests/harness-sites.mjs`, 280 m, 16.9 deg) now sits on the flank of the subtropical subsidence band
(dryBelt01 0.47 at 15-20 deg). After M1 its centre reads:

- biome 'Dry upland', not forestPotential
- effM 0.380 (rule: >= 0.6)
- eco class 5
- Tc margin 0.497 K (rule: >= 1 K)

All 96 ring samples are class 5, with effM 0.379-0.381 and no forest. Shipped values were effM 0.750 and class 6.

`test-results/climate-m1/site-disc.mjs` scanned the 4096 SITE_SEARCH points in the 100 km disc. Output:
`after/site-disc.json`.

| Disc count | Value |
|---|---|
| Land points | 3288 |
| Points with effM >= 0.6 | 0 |
| Points with forestPotential | 0 |
| effM p10 / p50 / p90 / max | 0.363 / 0.384 / 0.402 / 0.413 |
| Points in classes {4,5,6} | 1861 (all class 5) |
| Points also with Tc margin >= 1 K | 4 |
| Points passing every centre clause | 0 |

The disc is no longer forest, and the existing search finds no site. This is the brief's STOP condition. The two tests
in `tests/harness-sites.test.mjs` that assert a passing site exist fail on this branch, deliberately (see section 7).

Options for the owner (not taken here):

- (a') Widen the search disc, or search a different latitude band such as the 45-60 deg storm track or 0-8 deg near the
  ITCZ. This changes the search, not just its result.
- (b) Accept a site outside {4,5,6}.
- (c) Retune the band. This is rejected: physics is never retuned for a test.

The life and forest digests that read SITE_A (`life-cpu-digest`, `terrain-leaf-loop-digest`) still pass unmodified.
They pin rendered life at fixed inputs, not the site rule.

 /** Arithmetic smoothstep (no platform transcendental). */
 const smooth=(x:number,lo:number,hi:number)=>{const t=clamp01((x-lo)/(hi-lo));return t*t*(3-2*t);};
 
+/**
+ * surface-climate-2 positive-degree-day kernel. The year is the sinusoid
+ * T(t) = mu + A cos(2 pi t) (mu = annual mean, A = seasonal half-range). With
+ * v = (mu - thr)/A, the time-mean of max(0, T - thr) is A*H(v) and the
+ * fraction of the year with T > thr is G(v), where
+ *   H(v) = (sqrt(1-v^2) + v*acos(-v))/pi,   G(v) = acos(-v)/pi,
+ * H(v) = 0 and G(v) = 0 for v <= -1, H(v) = v and G(v) = 1 for v >= 1 (both
+ * continuous at +-1). Both are tabulated ONCE at module init on 513 nodes over
+ * v in [-1,1] (detAcos only here) and read with linear interpolation, so the
+ * per-sample path has no transcendental at all.
+ */
+export const KERNEL_STEPS=512;
+const H_TAB=new Float64Array(KERNEL_STEPS+1),G_TAB=new Float64Array(KERNEL_STEPS+1);
+for(let k=0;k<=KERNEL_STEPS;k++){
+  const v=-1+2*k/KERNEL_STEPS,a=detAcos(-v);
+  H_TAB[k]=(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-v*v))+v*a)/Math.PI;G_TAB[k]=a/Math.PI;
+}
+// Exact end nodes (H(-1) = G(-1) = 0, H(1) = G(1) = 1), whatever detAcos rounds to.
+H_TAB[0]=0;G_TAB[0]=0;H_TAB[KERNEL_STEPS]=1;G_TAB[KERNEL_STEPS]=1;
+/** Linear interpolation in a kernel table for v strictly inside (-1, 1). */
+function kernelAt(tab:Float64Array,v:number):number {
+  const f=(v+1)*(KERNEL_STEPS/2);
+  let i=Math.floor(f);if(i<0)i=0;else if(i>KERNEL_STEPS-1)i=KERNEL_STEPS-1;
+  return tab[i]!+(tab[i+1]!-tab[i]!)*(f-i);
+}
+/** The tabulated H(v) (mean positive excess per unit half-range). */
+export function pddKernelH(v:number):number {return v<=-1?0:v>=1?v:kernelAt(H_TAB,v);}
+/** The tabulated G(v) (fraction of the year above the threshold). */
+export function pddKernelG(v:number):number {return v<=-1?0:v>=1?1:kernelAt(G_TAB,v);}
+/**
+ * Degree-days (C*d per year) above `thresholdC` of a sinusoidal year with annual
+ * mean `annualK` (K) and half-range `halfK` (K). A < 1e-9 is a seasonless year.
+ */
+export function degreeDaysAbove(annualK:number,halfK:number,thresholdC:number):number {
+  const excess=annualK-273.15-thresholdC;
+  if(halfK<1e-9)return excess>0?excess*C.YEAR_D:0;
+  return C.YEAR_D*halfK*pddKernelH(excess/halfK);
+}

@@ -131,6 +190,36 @@ export function temperateHabitat(body:Body):boolean {

+/**
+ * surface-climate-2 step 2, warm-column lapse correction (a calibration choice,
+ * docs/surface-climate-v2.md): the lapse rate applied above the datum is
+ * lapse*f(T0), f = clamp(1 - WARM_COL_PER_K*(T0 - WARM_COL_REF_K), 0.8, 1.1),
+ * T0 the datum annual mean of the sample's latitude. Warm (moist, tropical)
+ * columns cool more slowly with height than the planet's mean lapse rate, cold
+ * columns faster. Without it the Earth equatorial snow line is 4302 m, below
+ * the 4450 m floor.
+ */
+export function warmColumnLapseFactor(datumAnnualK:number):number {
+  const f=1-C.WARM_COL_PER_K*(datumAnnualK-C.WARM_COL_REF_K);
+  return f<C.WARM_COL_MIN?C.WARM_COL_MIN:f>C.WARM_COL_MAX?C.WARM_COL_MAX:f;
+}
+
+/**
+ * surface-climate-2 step 3, warmest-month ceiling (symmetric): above
+ * TW_CEILING_K the raw warmest month TwRaw = annual + half saturates,
+ *   Tw' = 320 + e/(1 + e/40), e = TwRaw - 320   (Tw' < 360 K always),
+ * and the half-range becomes half' = Tw' - annual, so the coldest month
+ * annual - half' rises by the same amount and the annual mean is unchanged.
+ * half' is never negative (the warmest month is never below the annual mean);
+ * that floor binds only where the annual mean itself exceeds the saturated
+ * warmest month (a hot, nearly seasonless column; not a habitat world).
+ * PDD, treeline, growing season and the tile colour all read half'.
+ */
+export function warmestMonthCeilingK(rawWarmestK:number):number {
+  const e=rawWarmestK-C.TW_CEILING_K;
+  return e>0?C.TW_CEILING_K+e/(1+e/C.TW_CEILING_SCALE_K):rawWarmestK;
+}

+/**
+ * surface-climate-2 step 6, the ITCZ zonal band. Rows k = 0..ZONAL_ROWS over
+ * x = sin|latitude| = k/ZONAL_ROWS; LAT_DEG is their latitude (detAcos at module
+ * init only). Per forcing, with dI = |lat - itczLatDeg| (deg),
+ *   Z(dI) = 1 + A1 e^-(dI/W1)^2 + A2 e^-((dI-50)/W2)^2 - A3 e^-((dI-28)/W3)^2 - A4 e^-((dI-88)/W4)^2
+ * (ITCZ convergence, mid-latitude storm track, Hadley subsidence, polar desert;
+ * the squares are products and e^ is detExp) and the subsidence term
+ * e^-((dI-28)/W3)^2 alone is the dryBelt01 diagnostic. Both move with the ITCZ,
+ * so on a high-tilt world (ITCZ at the pole) the wet band is polar and the dry
+ * band follows it. Calibration: docs/surface-climate-v2.md.
+ */
+export const ZONAL_ROWS=512;
+const LAT_DEG=new Float64Array(ZONAL_ROWS+1);
+for(let k=0;k<=ZONAL_ROWS;k++)LAT_DEG[k]=90-detAcos(k/ZONAL_ROWS)*180/Math.PI;
+LAT_DEG[0]=0;LAT_DEG[ZONAL_ROWS]=90;
+const gauss=(x:number,w:number)=>{const u=x/w;return detExp(-(u*u));};
+/** The ITCZ row of a qMean table (first maximum) as sin(latitude). */
+export function itczSinLatOf(qMean:Float64Array):number {
+  let best=0;for(let k=1;k<=TABLE_ROWS;k++)if(qMean[k]!>qMean[best]!)best=k;
+  return best/TABLE_ROWS;
+}
+/** Zonal factor Z and subsidence diagnostic tables for an ITCZ latitude (deg). */
+export function zonalTables(itczLatDeg:number):{zonal:Float64Array;subsidence:Float64Array} {
+  const zonal=new Float64Array(ZONAL_ROWS+1),subsidence=new Float64Array(ZONAL_ROWS+1);
+  for(let k=0;k<=ZONAL_ROWS;k++){
+    const dI=LAT_DEG[k]!>itczLatDeg?LAT_DEG[k]!-itczLatDeg:itczLatDeg-LAT_DEG[k]!;
+    const sub=gauss(dI-C.ZONAL_C3,C.ZONAL_W3);
+    zonal[k]=1+C.ZONAL_A1*gauss(dI,C.ZONAL_W1)+C.ZONAL_A2*gauss(dI-C.ZONAL_C2,C.ZONAL_W2)-C.ZONAL_A3*sub-C.ZONAL_A4*gauss(dI-C.ZONAL_C4,C.ZONAL_W4);
+    subsidence[k]=sub;
+  }
+  return {zonal,subsidence};
+}

+// BIOME2-CLIMATE M1 acceptance 2 (plan v2 P1 step 6): the polar-ITCZ branch is a main path. Corpus = the
+// W0-CLIMATE definition (temperate-habitat bodies of the first 2000 systems from sector x = 266 of LETHRA-001,
+// land share >= 1 % on 8000 Fibonacci directions, level-0 sampler). Selected: ITCZ (argmax row of the annual
+// insolation table) poleward of 45 deg. Judged: >= 50 land samples both within 10 deg of the ITCZ and 18-35 deg
+// from it. Every judged planet must have mean effective moisture near the ITCZ >= in its subsidence band.
+// Negative: the shipped surface-climate-1.1 rule (legacyMoisture - 0.0425 x fixed sin-lat 0.18-0.52 belt; legacy
+// noise is unchanged by M1) fails it on many planets (step-0 baseline: 93 of 213 judged).
+test('polar ITCZ: every judged corpus planet is wetter near the ITCZ than 18-35 deg from it (shipped belt fails)', t => {
+  const g0 = galaxies('LETHRA-001')[0], CN = 8000, D = 180 / Math.PI;
+  const fibN = i => { const y = 1 - 2 * (i + .5) / CN, p = i * 2.399963229728653, r = Math.sqrt(1 - y * y); return [Math.cos(p) * r, y, Math.sin(p) * r]; };
+  const sm = (x, lo, hi) => { const u = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (x - lo) / (hi - lo))); return u * u * (3 - 2 * u); };
+  const shippedBelt = x => sm(x, .15, .21) * (1 - sm(x, .49, .55));
+  const corpus = []; let systems = 0;
+  for (let sx = 266; systems < 2000; sx++) for (const ref of sector(g0, [sx, 0, 0]).refs) {
+    if (systems >= 2000) break; systems++;
+    for (const b of system(ref).planets) if (temperateHabitat(b)) corpus.push(b);
+  }
+  let selected = 0, judged = 0, shippedFails = 0; const fails = [];
+  for (const b of corpus) {
+    const f = climateForcingFor(b);
+    if (!(f.itczLatDeg > 45)) continue; // not selected; land share is only needed for selected bodies
+    const ts = statisticSampler(b), c = surfaceClimate(f), s = createClimateSample();
+    let land = 0; const near = [0, 0, 0], sub = [0, 0, 0];
+    for (let i = 0; i < CN; i++) {
+      const d = fibN(i), x = ts(d); if (x.ocean) continue; land++;
+      c(d, x.groundHeightM, s);
+      const ax = Math.min(1, Math.abs(d[1])), dI = Math.abs(Math.asin(ax) * D - f.itczLatDeg);
+      const shipped = s.legacyMoisture - .0425 * shippedBelt(ax);
+      if (dI <= 10) { near[0]++; near[1] += s.effectiveMoisture; near[2] += shipped; }
+      else if (dI >= 18 && dI <= 35) { sub[0]++; sub[1] += s.effectiveMoisture; sub[2] += shipped; }
+    }
+    if (100 * land / CN < 1) continue;
+    selected++;
+    if (near[0] < 50 || sub[0] < 50) continue;
+    judged++;
+    if (!(near[1] / near[0] >= sub[1] / sub[0])) fails.push({ id: b.id, near: near[1] / near[0], sub: sub[1] / sub[0] });
+    if (!(near[2] / near[0] >= sub[2] / sub[0])) shippedFails++;
+  }
+  t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify({ corpus: corpus.length, selected, judged, fails: fails.length, shippedFails }));
+  assert.equal(selected, 311, 'selected polar-ITCZ planets (plan v2: 311)');
+  assert.equal(judged, 213, 'judged planets (>= 50 land samples in both bands)');
+  assert.deepEqual(fails, [], 'every judged planet passes');
+  assert.ok(shippedFails >= 50, `negative: the shipped fixed belt fails ${shippedFails} judged planets (baseline 93)`);
+});

+// surface-climate-2 Earth reference (backlog #9 BIOME2-CLIMATE M1, plan
+// docs/biome-climate-increment-2-plan-v2-2026-10-04.json P1-CLIMATE acceptance 1;
+// design and sources: docs/surface-climate-v2.md).
+//
+// The Earth reference body: 288 K, obliquity 23.44 deg, water 0.7, g 9.81 m/s^2
+// (lapse 6.4996 K/km). ELA = the lowest height with snowFraction > 0.6, the
+// classifier's 'Alpine / polar snow' label (PDD < 190 C*d under PDD_CRIT 250 /
+// PDD_RAMP 100). Bands: equator >= 4450 m, 45 deg 2700-3100 m, 60 deg
+// 1200-1700 m, 70 deg 550-1000 m, 78 deg 250-650 m.
+import {test} from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {climateForcingFromParams,surfaceClimate,createClimateSample,degreeDaysAbove,warmColumnLapseFactor,itczSinLatOf,insolationTables,ZONAL_ROWS,CLIMATE_CALIBRATION as C} from '../src/surface-climate.ts';
+
+const D=Math.PI/180;
+export const EARTH={anchorTempK:288,obliquityRad:23.44*D,water:.7,surfaceGravityMS2:9.81,moistureSeed:1};
+export const ELA_BANDS=Object.freeze({0:[4450,Infinity],45:[2700,3100],60:[1200,1700],70:[550,1000],78:[250,650]});
+
+const at=lat=>[Math.cos(lat*D),Math.sin(lat*D),0];
+/** Lowest height (m, 0..20 km, bisection to < 1 m) where snowAt(lat, h) holds; 0 if it already holds at the datum. */
+export function elaM(snowAt,lat){
+  if(snowAt(lat,0))return 0;
+  let lo=0,hi=20000;
+  if(!snowAt(lat,hi))return Infinity;
+  while(hi-lo>.5){const m=(lo+hi)/2;if(snowAt(lat,m))hi=m;else lo=m;}
+  return Math.round(hi);
+}
+const inBand=(lat,h)=>h>=ELA_BANDS[lat][0]&&h<=ELA_BANDS[lat][1];
+
+const forcing=climateForcingFromParams(EARTH),climate=surfaceClimate(forcing),o=createClimateSample();
+/** The live rule: the classifier label, snowFraction > 0.6. */
+const liveLabel=(lat,h)=>climate(at(lat),h,o).snowFraction>.6;
+
+test('Earth reference: the PDD snow line (label) lies in every Earth ELA band',t=>{
+  const rows=Object.keys(ELA_BANDS).map(Number).map(lat=>({lat,elaM:elaM(liveLabel,lat)}));
+  t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify(rows));
+  // Plan v2 prototype M1 (label): 4608 / 3003 / 1505 / 719 / 426 m.
+  for(const {lat,elaM:h} of rows)assert.ok(inBand(lat,h),`lat ${lat}: ELA ${h} m outside ${ELA_BANDS[lat]}`);
+  // The label is monotone in height at every tested latitude (no snow below the ELA).
+  for(const {lat,elaM:h} of rows)for(let z=0;z<h;z+=97)assert.equal(liveLabel(lat,z),false,`lat ${lat} h ${z}`);
+});
+
+test('negative: the shipped annual-mean rule (surface-climate-1, T_SNOW 268 K, ramp 6 K) fails the 60 deg band',()=>{

+  // annual(h) = annual(0) - lapse*h, snow = clamp01((268 - annual)/6).
+  const shipped=thr=>(lat,h)=>{climate(at(lat),0,o);const annual=o.annualMeanTempK-forcing.lapseKPerM*h;return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(268-annual)/6))>thr;};
+  const label=elaM(shipped(.6),60),onset=elaM(shipped(0),60);

+test('warm-column lapse: the factor, its clamps, and why it is mandatory (equator without it < 4450 m)',()=>{

+});
+
+// ---------------- step 6: the ITCZ zonal band ----------------
+const fib=(i,n)=>{const y=1-2*(i+.5)/n,p=i*2.399963229728653,r=Math.sqrt(1-y*y);return [Math.cos(p)*r,y,Math.sin(p)*r];};
+/** Zonal means relative to the ITCZ over N Fibonacci directions at the datum (the reference has no terrain). */
+export function zonalBands(f,N=20000,effOf=null){
+  const c=surfaceClimate(f),s=createClimateSample(),B={itcz:[0,10],sub:[15,35],dry:[18,32],mid:[45,60],pol:[75,90.1]},acc={};
+  for(const k in B)acc[k]={n:0,P:0,eff:0};
+  const bins=Array.from({length:18},()=>({n:0,P:0,eff:0}));
+  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
+    const d=fib(i,N);c(d,0,s);
+    const lat=Math.asin(Math.abs(d[1]))/D,dI=Math.abs(lat-f.itczLatDeg),eff=effOf?effOf(s,lat):s.effectiveMoisture;
+    for(const k in B)if(dI>=B[k][0]&&dI<B[k][1]){acc[k].n++;acc[k].P+=s.precipMm;acc[k].eff+=eff;}
+    const b=bins[Math.min(17,Math.floor(lat/5))];b.n++;b.P+=s.precipMm;b.eff+=eff;
+  }
+  const m=k=>({P:acc[k].P/acc[k].n,eff:acc[k].eff/acc[k].n});
+  return {itcz:m('itcz'),sub:m('sub'),dry:m('dry'),mid:m('mid'),pol:m('pol'),bins:bins.map((b,k)=>({lat:k*5+2.5,P:b.P/b.n,eff:b.eff/b.n}))};
+}
+
+test('ITCZ: argmax row of the annual insolation table; equator below ~54 deg obliquity, pole above',()=>{

+  assert.equal(forcing.itczSinLat,0);assert.equal(forcing.itczLatDeg,0);assert.equal(forcing.hadleyEdgeDeg,30);

+});

+  assert.ok(r.effDry<=.4,`effM(18-32) = ${r.effDry}`);
+  assert.ok(r.effMid>=.45&&r.effMid<=.65,`effM(45-60) = ${r.effMid}`);
+});
+
+test('negative: the uncalibrated starting band (P_REF 1000, a = .9/.6/.55/.4, w = 10/12/9/12) misses the Earth targets',()=>{
+  // Re-statement of plan v1's starting band on the live temperature field: effM = AI/(AI+0.6),

+    const P=1000*Math.max(.2,1+.025*(s.annualMeanTempK-288))*Z0(lat)*(.4+1.2*s.legacyMoisture),ai=P/pet;return ai/(ai+.6);});

+
+test('high-tilt synthetic world (70 deg): the wettest band is polar and the driest band moves with the ITCZ',t=>{

+test('PDD kernel: tabulated H and G match the closed form; degree-days of a sinusoidal year',()=>{
+  // Independent platform-math reference of the sinusoidal-year kernel.
+  const H=v=>v<=-1?0:v>=1?v:(Math.sqrt(1-v*v)+v*Math.acos(-v))/Math.PI,G=v=>v<=-1?0:v>=1?1:Math.acos(-v)/Math.PI;
+  assert.equal(KERNEL_STEPS,512,'513 nodes over [-1, 1]');
+  let eH=0,eG=0,prevH=-1,prevG=-1;
+  for(let i=0;i<=100000;i++){
+    const v=-1.2+2.4*i/100000,h=pddKernelH(v),g=pddKernelG(v);
+    eH=Math.max(eH,Math.abs(h-H(v)));eG=Math.max(eG,Math.abs(g-G(v)));
+    assert.ok(h>=prevH&&g>=prevG,`kernel is monotone at v=${v}`);prevH=h;prevG=g;
+  }
+  // Measured: H 1.09e-5 (worst next to v = 1), G 7.0e-3 (the sqrt singularity at +-1).
+  assert.ok(eH<2e-5,`H max error ${eH}`);assert.ok(eG<1e-2,`G max error ${eG}`);
+  for(const v of [-1,1])assert.equal(pddKernelH(v),H(v));
+  assert.equal(pddKernelG(-1),0);assert.equal(pddKernelG(1),1);assert.equal(pddKernelH(0),H(0));
+  // Degree-days: a seasonless year, a year entirely above and entirely below the threshold, and the mid case.
+  assert.equal(degreeDaysAbove(283.15,0,0),10*365.25);assert.equal(degreeDaysAbove(263.15,0,0),0);
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(degreeDaysAbove(293.15,5,0)-20*365.25)<1e-9,'never below 0 C: PDD = annual excess x year');
+  assert.equal(degreeDaysAbove(253.15,5,0),0,'never above 0 C');
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(degreeDaysAbove(273.15,10,0)-365.25*10/Math.PI)<1e-9,'mean at 0 C: A/pi per day');
+  // Against a brute-force integral of max(0, mu + A cos t) over 100k time steps.
+  for(const [mu,A,thr] of [[268,12,0],[275,20,0],[290,9,30],[300,18,30],[262,25,0]]){
+    let s=0;const n=100000;for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const T=mu-273.15+A*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*(k+.5)/n)-thr;if(T>0)s+=T;}
+    const brute=s/n*365.25;
+    assert.ok(Math.abs(degreeDaysAbove(mu,A,thr)-brute)<=365.25*A*2e-5+1e-6,`mu ${mu} A ${A} thr ${thr}: ${degreeDaysAbove(mu,A,thr)} vs ${brute}`);
+  }

+test('PDD kernel: tabulated H and G match the closed form; degree-days of a sinusoidal year',()=>{
+  // Independent platform-math reference of the sinusoidal-year kernel.
+  const H=v=>v<=-1?0:v>=1?v:(Math.sqrt(1-v*v)+v*Math.acos(-v))/Math.PI,G=v=>v<=-1?0:v>=1?1:Math.acos(-v)/Math.PI;
+  assert.equal(KERNEL_STEPS,512,'513 nodes over [-1, 1]');
+  let eH=0,eG=0,prevH=-1,prevG=-1;
+  for(let i=0;i<=100000;i++){
+    const v=-1.2+2.4*i/100000,h=pddKernelH(v),g=pddKernelG(v);
+    eH=Math.max(eH,Math.abs(h-H(v)));eG=Math.max(eG,Math.abs(g-G(v)));
+    assert.ok(h>=prevH&&g>=prevG,`kernel is monotone at v=${v}`);prevH=h;prevG=g;
+  }
+  // Measured: H 1.09e-5 (worst next to v = 1), G 7.0e-3 (the sqrt singularity at +-1).
+  assert.ok(eH<2e-5,`H max error ${eH}`);assert.ok(eG<1e-2,`G max error ${eG}`);
+  for(const v of [-1,1])assert.equal(pddKernelH(v),H(v));
+  assert.equal(pddKernelG(-1),0);assert.equal(pddKernelG(1),1);assert.equal(pddKernelH(0),H(0));
+  // Degree-days: a seasonless year, a year entirely above and entirely below the threshold, and the mid case.
+  assert.equal(degreeDaysAbove(283.15,0,0),10*365.25);assert.equal(degreeDaysAbove(263.15,0,0),0);
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(degreeDaysAbove(293.15,5,0)-20*365.25)<1e-9,'never below 0 C: PDD = annual excess x year');
+  assert.equal(degreeDaysAbove(253.15,5,0),0,'never above 0 C');
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(degreeDaysAbove(273.15,10,0)-365.25*10/Math.PI)<1e-9,'mean at 0 C: A/pi per day');
+  // Against a brute-force integral of max(0, mu + A cos t) over 100k time steps.
+  for(const [mu,A,thr] of [[268,12,0],[275,20,0],[290,9,30],[300,18,30],[262,25,0]]){
+    let s=0;const n=100000;for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const T=mu-273.15+A*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*(k+.5)/n)-thr;if(T>0)s+=T;}
+    const brute=s/n*365.25;
+    assert.ok(Math.abs(degreeDaysAbove(mu,A,thr)-brute)<=365.25*A*2e-5+1e-6,`mu ${mu} A ${A} thr ${thr}: ${degreeDaysAbove(mu,A,thr)} vs ${brute}`);
+  }

// R5-ATLAS-CLIMATE-M1 step 6 calibration, round 2 (plain JS probe, Math.* allowed; NOT shipped code).
//
// Round 1 (calibrate-zonal.mjs, output calibrate-zonal.out.jsonl) scanned the zonal band amplitudes,
// the subsidence width and PET_MIN with the precipitation scale fixed at 1000 mm: 0 of 7776 candidates
// met the v1 P1 step-6 Earth targets. The reason is structural: with Z = 1 + (bumps) the mid-latitude
// band keeps Z >= ~1, and at 1000 mm its aridity index (P / Holdridge PET ~450 mm) is ~1.6-2,
// i.e. effM ~0.75-0.8, above the 0.45-0.65 target, while the 1.2x P(45-60)/P(15-35) target forbids
// lowering it by a mid-latitude dip. Round 2 therefore also scans the precipitation scale P_REF_MM
// (the one number that sets P against PET) and the widths, and keeps the formula of plan v1 step 5/6.
//
// Inputs: ONLY the Earth reference (288 K, 23.44 deg, water 0.7, g 9.81, h = 0, all directions,
// noise included). No catalogue planet, SITE_A or corpus number is used. The live climate supplies
// annual mean, half-range (after the step-3 ceiling) and legacy noise per direction.
// Targets (v1 plan P1 step 6): P(15-35) <= 0.55 P(0-10); P(45-60) >= 1.2 P(15-35); P(>75) < 0.35 P(0-10);
// effM(0-10) >= 0.6; effM(18-32) <= 0.40; effM(45-60) in 0.45-0.65. Margin required: >= 0.02 each.
// Choice rule: among passing candidates, the one closest to the plan's starting band
// (a = 0.9, 0.6, 0.55, 0.4; w = 10, 12, 9, 12; PET_MIN 120; P_REF 1000).
// Usage: node test-results/climate-m1/calibrate-zonal-2.mjs [--eval a1,a2,a3,a4,w1,w2,w3,w4,petMin,pRef]
import * as SC from '../../src/surface-climate.ts';
const D=180/Math.PI,N=+(process.env.N||20000);
const fib=(i,n)=>{const y=1-2*(i+.5)/n,p=i*2.399963229728653,r=Math.sqrt(1-y*y);return [Math.cos(p)*r,y,Math.sin(p)*r];};
const f=SC.climateForcingFromParams({anchorTempK:288,obliquityRad:23.44/D,water:.7,surfaceGravityMS2:9.81,moistureSeed:1});
const c=SC.surfaceClimate(f),s=SC.createClimateSample();
const H=v=>v<=-1?0:v>=1?v:(Math.sqrt(1-v*v)+v*Math.acos(-v))/Math.PI;
const dd=(thr,mu,A)=>A<1e-9?Math.max(0,mu-thr)*365.25:365.25*A*H((mu-thr)/A);
// Per-direction, parameter-free precomputation: |lat|, biotemp PET (unfloored), P without Z and scale.
const lat=new Float64Array(N),petRaw=new Float64Array(N),pBase=new Float64Array(N);
for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
  const d=fib(i,N);c(d,0,s);
  const mu=s.annualMeanTempK-273.15,A=s.seasonalHalfRangeK;
  lat[i]=Math.asin(Math.abs(d[1]))*D;
  petRaw[i]=58.93*(dd(0,mu,A)-dd(30,mu,A))/365.25;
  pBase[i]=Math.min(1.2,Math.max(.2,.7/.7))*Math.max(.2,1+.025*(s.annualMeanTempK-288))*(.4+1.2*s.legacyMoisture);
}
const g=(x,w)=>Math.exp(-(x/w)*(x/w));
const BANDS={itcz:[0,10],sub:[15,35],dry:[18,32],mid:[45,60],pol:[75,90.1]};
function evalParams([a1,a2,a3,a4,w1,w2,w3,w4,petMin,pRef]){
  const acc={};for(const k in BANDS)acc[k]={n:0,P:0,eff:0};
  let zMin=1e9;
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
    const x=lat[i],z=1+a1*g(x,w1)+a2*g(x-50,w2)-a3*g(x-28,w3)-a4*g(x-88,w4);if(z<zMin)zMin=z;
    const P=pRef*pBase[i]*z,PET=Math.max(petMin,petRaw[i]),AI=P/PET,eff=AI/(AI+.6);
    for(const k in BANDS){const [lo,hi]=BANDS[k];if(x>=lo&&x<hi){acc[k].n++;acc[k].P+=P;acc[k].eff+=eff;}}
  }
  const m=k=>({P:acc[k].P/acc[k].n,eff:acc[k].eff/acc[k].n});
  const it=m('itcz'),sub=m('sub'),dry=m('dry'),mid=m('mid'),pol=m('pol');
  const t={subOverItcz:sub.P/it.P,midOverSub:mid.P/sub.P,polOverItcz:pol.P/it.P,effItcz:it.eff,effDry:dry.eff,effMid:mid.eff,zMin};
  const margins=[.55-t.subOverItcz,t.midOverSub-1.2,.35-t.polOverItcz,t.effItcz-.6,.40-t.effDry,t.effMid-.45,.65-t.effMid];
  return {t,margins,minMargin:Math.min(...margins)};
}

+// BIOME2-CLIMATE M1 acceptance 2 (plan v2 P1 step 6): the polar-ITCZ branch is a main path. Corpus = the
+// W0-CLIMATE definition (temperate-habitat bodies of the first 2000 systems from sector x = 266 of LETHRA-001,
+// land share >= 1 % on 8000 Fibonacci directions, level-0 sampler). Selected: ITCZ (argmax row of the annual
+// insolation table) poleward of 45 deg. Judged: >= 50 land samples both within 10 deg of the ITCZ and 18-35 deg
+// from it. Every judged planet must have mean effective moisture near the ITCZ >= in its subsidence band.
+// Negative: the shipped surface-climate-1.1 rule (legacyMoisture - 0.0425 x fixed sin-lat 0.18-0.52 belt; legacy
+// noise is unchanged by M1) fails it on many planets (step-0 baseline: 93 of 213 judged).
+test('polar ITCZ: every judged corpus planet is wetter near the ITCZ than 18-35 deg from it (shipped belt fails)', t => {
+  const g0 = galaxies('LETHRA-001')[0], CN = 8000, D = 180 / Math.PI;
+  const fibN = i => { const y = 1 - 2 * (i + .5) / CN, p = i * 2.399963229728653, r = Math.sqrt(1 - y * y); return [Math.cos(p) * r, y, Math.sin(p) * r]; };
+  const sm = (x, lo, hi) => { const u = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (x - lo) / (hi - lo))); return u * u * (3 - 2 * u); };
+  const shippedBelt = x => sm(x, .15, .21) * (1 - sm(x, .49, .55));
+  const corpus = []; let systems = 0;
+  for (let sx = 266; systems < 2000; sx++) for (const ref of sector(g0, [sx, 0, 0]).refs) {
+    if (systems >= 2000) break; systems++;
+    for (const b of system(ref).planets) if (temperateHabitat(b)) corpus.push(b);
+  }
+  let selected = 0, judged = 0, shippedFails = 0; const fails = [];
+  for (const b of corpus) {
+    const f = climateForcingFor(b);
+    if (!(f.itczLatDeg > 45)) continue; // not selected; land share is only needed for selected bodies
+    const ts = statisticSampler(b), c = surfaceClimate(f), s = createClimateSample();
+    let land = 0; const near = [0, 0, 0], sub = [0, 0, 0];
+    for (let i = 0; i < CN; i++) {
+      const d = fibN(i), x = ts(d); if (x.ocean) continue; land++;
+      c(d, x.groundHeightM, s);
+      const ax = Math.min(1, Math.abs(d[1])), dI = Math.abs(Math.asin(ax) * D - f.itczLatDeg);
+      const shipped = s.legacyMoisture - .0425 * shippedBelt(ax);
+      if (dI <= 10) { near[0]++; near[1] += s.effectiveMoisture; near[2] += shipped; }
+      else if (dI >= 18 && dI <= 35) { sub[0]++; sub[1] += s.effectiveMoisture; sub[2] += shipped; }
+    }
+    if (100 * land / CN < 1) continue;
+    selected++;
+    if (near[0] < 50 || sub[0] < 50) continue;
+    judged++;
+    if (!(near[1] / near[0] >= sub[1] / sub[0])) fails.push({ id: b.id, near: near[1] / near[0], sub: sub[1] / sub[0] });
+    if (!(near[2] / near[0] >= sub[2] / sub[0])) shippedFails++;
+  }
+  t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify({ corpus: corpus.length, selected, judged, fails: fails.length, shippedFails }));
+  assert.equal(selected, 311, 'selected polar-ITCZ planets (plan v2: 311)');
+  assert.equal(judged, 213, 'judged planets (>= 50 land samples in both bands)');
+  assert.deepEqual(fails, [], 'every judged planet passes');
+  assert.ok(shippedFails >= 50, `negative: the shipped fixed belt fails ${shippedFails} judged planets (baseline 93)`);
+});

+//
+// The Earth reference body: 288 K, obliquity 23.44 deg, water 0.7, g 9.81 m/s^2
+// (lapse 6.4996 K/km). ELA = the lowest height with snowFraction > 0.6, the
+// classifier's 'Alpine / polar snow' label (PDD < 190 C*d under PDD_CRIT 250 /
+// PDD_RAMP 100). Bands: equator >= 4450 m, 45 deg 2700-3100 m, 60 deg
+// 1200-1700 m, 70 deg 550-1000 m, 78 deg 250-650 m.
+import {test} from 'node:test';
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {climateForcingFromParams,surfaceClimate,createClimateSample,degreeDaysAbove,warmColumnLapseFactor,itczSinLatOf,insolationTables,ZONAL_ROWS,CLIMATE_CALIBRATION as C} from '../src/surface-climate.ts';
+
+const D=Math.PI/180;
+export const EARTH={anchorTempK:288,obliquityRad:23.44*D,water:.7,surfaceGravityMS2:9.81,moistureSeed:1};
+export const ELA_BANDS=Object.freeze({0:[4450,Infinity],45:[2700,3100],60:[1200,1700],70:[550,1000],78:[250,650]});
+
+const at=lat=>[Math.cos(lat*D),Math.sin(lat*D),0];
+/** Lowest height (m, 0..20 km, bisection to < 1 m) where snowAt(lat, h) holds; 0 if it already holds at the datum. */
+export function elaM(snowAt,lat){
+  if(snowAt(lat,0))return 0;
+  let lo=0,hi=20000;
+  if(!snowAt(lat,hi))return Infinity;
+  while(hi-lo>.5){const m=(lo+hi)/2;if(snowAt(lat,m))hi=m;else lo=m;}
+  return Math.round(hi);
+}
+const inBand=(lat,h)=>h>=ELA_BANDS[lat][0]&&h<=ELA_BANDS[lat][1];
+
+const forcing=climateForcingFromParams(EARTH),climate=surfaceClimate(forcing),o=createClimateSample();
+/** The live rule: the classifier label, snowFraction > 0.6. */
+const liveLabel=(lat,h)=>climate(at(lat),h,o).snowFraction>.6;
+
+test('Earth reference: the PDD snow line (label) lies in every Earth ELA band',t=>{
+  const rows=Object.keys(ELA_BANDS).map(Number).map(lat=>({lat,elaM:elaM(liveLabel,lat)}));
+  t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify(rows));
+  // Plan v2 prototype M1 (label): 4608 / 3003 / 1505 / 719 / 426 m.
+  for(const {lat,elaM:h} of rows)assert.ok(inBand(lat,h),`lat ${lat}: ELA ${h} m outside ${ELA_BANDS[lat]}`);
+  // The label is monotone in height at every tested latitude (no snow below the ELA).
+  for(const {lat,elaM:h} of rows)for(let z=0;z<h;z+=97)assert.equal(liveLabel(lat,z),false,`lat ${lat} h ${z}`);
+});
+
+test('negative: the shipped annual-mean rule (surface-climate-1, T_SNOW 268 K, ramp 6 K) fails the 60 deg band',()=>{
+  // Independent re-statement of the surface-climate-1 snow rule on the same datum column:
+  // annual(h) = annual(0) - lapse*h, snow = clamp01((268 - annual)/6).
+  const shipped=thr=>(lat,h)=>{climate(at(lat),0,o);const annual=o.annualMeanTempK-forcing.lapseKPerM*h;return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(268-annual)/6))>thr;};
+  const label=elaM(shipped(.6),60),onset=elaM(shipped(0),60);
+  // Plan v2 measuredBaseline: 810 m label / 256 m onset.
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(label-810)<=2&&Math.abs(onset-256)<=2,`shipped rule ${label} / ${onset} m`);
+  assert.ok(!inBand(60,label)&&!inBand(60,onset),'the annual-mean rule must fail the 60 deg band');
+});
+
+test('warm-column lapse: the factor, its clamps, and why it is mandatory (equator without it < 4450 m)',()=>{
+  assert.equal(warmColumnLapseFactor(288),1);
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(warmColumnLapseFactor(308)-.9)<1e-12&&Math.abs(warmColumnLapseFactor(268)-1.1)<1e-12);
+  assert.equal(warmColumnLapseFactor(400),C.WARM_COL_MIN);assert.equal(warmColumnLapseFactor(150),C.WARM_COL_MAX);
+  // The live column: annual(h) = annual(0) - lapse*f(annual(0))*h, half unchanged with height.
+  climate(at(0),0,o);const T0=o.annualMeanTempK,half=o.seasonalHalfRangeK;
+  climate(at(0),3000,o);assert.ok(Math.abs(o.annualMeanTempK-(T0-forcing.lapseKPerM*warmColumnLapseFactor(T0)*3000))<1e-9);
+  // Negative: the same PDD rule with the plain lapse (factor 1) puts the equatorial label at 4302 m (plan v2 R8).
+  const plain=(lat,h)=>{climate(at(lat),0,o);return degreeDaysAbove(o.annualMeanTempK-forcing.lapseKPerM*h,o.seasonalHalfRangeK,0)<C.PDD_CRIT-.6*C.PDD_RAMP;};
+  const eq=elaM(plain,0);
+  assert.ok(Math.abs(eq-4302)<=2,`no warm column: ${eq} m`);
+  assert.ok(!inBand(0,eq),'without the warm-column factor the equator fails the >= 4450 m band');
+  assert.ok(half>0);
+});
+
+// ---------------- step 6: the ITCZ zonal band ----------------
+const fib=(i,n)=>{const y=1-2*(i+.5)/n,p=i*2.399963229728653,r=Math.sqrt(1-y*y);return [Math.cos(p)*r,y,Math.sin(p)*r];};
+/** Zonal means relative to the ITCZ over N Fibonacci directions at the datum (the reference has no terrain). */
+export function zonalBands(f,N=20000,effOf=null){
+  const c=surfaceClimate(f),s=createClimateSample(),B={itcz:[0,10],sub:[15,35],dry:[18,32],mid:[45,60],pol:[75,90.1]},acc={};
+  for(const k in B)acc[k]={n:0,P:0,eff:0};
+  const bins=Array.from({length:18},()=>({n:0,P:0,eff:0}));
+  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
+    const d=fib(i,N);c(d,0,s);
+    const lat=Math.asin(Math.abs(d[1]))/D,dI=Math.abs(lat-f.itczLatDeg),eff=effOf?effOf(s,lat):s.effectiveMoisture;
+    for(const k in B)if(dI>=B[k][0]&&dI<B[k][1]){acc[k].n++;acc[k].P+=s.precipMm;acc[k].eff+=eff;}
+    const b=bins[Math.min(17,Math.floor(lat/5))];b.n++;b.P+=s.precipMm;b.eff+=eff;
+  }
+  const m=k=>({P:acc[k].P/acc[k].n,eff:acc[k].eff/acc[k].n});
+  return {itcz:m('itcz'),sub:m('sub'),dry:m('dry'),mid:m('mid'),pol:m('pol'),bins:bins.map((b,k)=>({lat:k*5+2.5,P:b.P/b.n,eff:b.eff/b.n}))};
+}
+
+test('ITCZ: argmax row of the annual insolation table; equator below ~54 deg obliquity, pole above',()=>{
+  assert.equal(ZONAL_ROWS,512);
+  for(const [deg,itcz] of [[0,0],[23.44,0],[45,0],[53,0],[56,1],[61.4,1],[70,1],[89.8,1]])
+    assert.equal(itczSinLatOf(insolationTables(deg*D).qMean),itcz,`obliquity ${deg}`);
+  assert.equal(forcing.itczSinLat,0);assert.equal(forcing.itczLatDeg,0);assert.equal(forcing.hadleyEdgeDeg,30);
+  const f70=climateForcingFromParams({...EARTH,obliquityRad:70*D});
+  assert.equal(f70.itczSinLat,1);assert.equal(f70.itczLatDeg,90);
+});
+
+test('Earth reference zonal targets (calibrated band): P ratios and effective moisture per band',t=>{
+  const z=zonalBands(forcing);
+  const r={subOverItcz:z.sub.P/z.itcz.P,midOverSub:z.mid.P/z.sub.P,polOverItcz:z.pol.P/z.itcz.P,effItcz:z.itcz.eff,effDry:z.dry.eff,effMid:z.mid.eff};
+  t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify(r));
+  assert.ok(r.subOverItcz<=.55,`P(15-35) / P(0-10) = ${r.subOverItcz}`);
+  assert.ok(r.midOverSub>=1.2,`P(45-60) / P(15-35) = ${r.midOverSub}`);
+  assert.ok(r.polOverItcz<.35,`P(>75) / P(0-10) = ${r.polOverItcz}`);
+  assert.ok(r.effItcz>=.6,`effM(0-10) = ${r.effItcz}`);
+  assert.ok(r.effDry<=.4,`effM(18-32) = ${r.effDry}`);
+  assert.ok(r.effMid>=.45&&r.effMid<=.65,`effM(45-60) = ${r.effMid}`);
+});
+
+test('negative: the uncalibrated starting band (P_REF 1000, a = .9/.6/.55/.4, w = 10/12/9/12) misses the Earth targets',()=>{
+  // Re-statement of plan v1's starting band on the live temperature field: effM = AI/(AI+0.6),
+  // AI = P/max(120, 58.93 biotemp), P = 1000 x pTemp x Z0 x (0.4 + 1.2 noise).
+  const g=(x,w)=>Math.exp(-(x/w)*(x/w)),Z0=dI=>1+.9*g(dI,10)+.6*g(dI-50,12)-.55*g(dI-28,9)-.4*g(dI-88,12);
+  const z=zonalBands(forcing,20000,(s,lat)=>{
+    const pet=Math.max(120,58.93*(degreeDaysAbove(s.annualMeanTempK,s.seasonalHalfRangeK,0)-degreeDaysAbove(s.annualMeanTempK,s.seasonalHalfRangeK,30))/365.25);
+    const P=1000*Math.max(.2,1+.025*(s.annualMeanTempK-288))*Z0(lat)*(.4+1.2*s.legacyMoisture),ai=P/pet;return ai/(ai+.6);});
+  // Measured: effM(18-32) 0.472 (> 0.40), effM(45-60) 0.799 (> 0.65).
+  assert.ok(z.dry.eff>.4&&z.mid.eff>.65,`uncalibrated: dry ${z.dry.eff}, mid ${z.mid.eff}`);
+});
+
+test('high-tilt synthetic world (70 deg): the wettest band is polar and the driest band moves with the ITCZ',t=>{
+  const f70=climateForcingFromParams({...EARTH,obliquityRad:70*D});
+  const z=zonalBands(f70);
+  const wettest=z.bins.reduce((a,b)=>b.P>a.P?b:a),driest=z.bins.reduce((a,b)=>b.eff<a.eff?b:a);
+  t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify({wettestLat:wettest.lat,driestLat:driest.lat,effItcz:z.itcz.eff,effSub:z.sub.eff}));
+  assert.ok(wettest.lat>=75,`wettest 5 deg band at ${wettest.lat}`);
+  const dI=90-driest.lat;assert.ok(dI>=18&&dI<=35,`driest band ${dI} deg from the ITCZ`);
+  assert.ok(z.itcz.eff>z.sub.eff,'near the ITCZ wetter than 15-35 deg from it');
+  // The shipped fixed belt's negative is the corpus polar-ITCZ test (tests/acceptance/climate-agreement.test.mjs):
+  // on this synthetic world its belt deficit (0.0425) is below the legacy noise, so a band minimum does not locate it.
+});

// Deterministic search and robustness evaluation for the shared harness
// living site (tests/harness-sites.mjs). Not a test file; used by
// tests/harness-sites.test.mjs and by whoever has to move SITE_A after a
// terrain change. Reads the merged terrain/classifier at the harness date;
// never tunes either.
import {galaxies,refByAddress,system} from '../../src/catalog.ts';
import {parseAddress} from '../../src/address.ts';
import {starLifecycle,planetLifecycle} from '../../src/stellar-evolution.ts';
import {planetSnapshotAt} from '../../src/world-surface.ts';
import {CURRENT_VERSIONS} from '../../src/time-nav.ts';
import {terrainSampler} from '../../src/terrain.ts';
import {biomeClassifier} from '../../src/surface-biomes.ts';
import {HARNESS_SEED,HARNESS_SYSTEM_ID,HARNESS_BODY_ID,HARNESS_DATE,SITE_RULE,SITE_SEARCH,SITE_A_LEGACY} from '../harness-sites.mjs';

const norm=a=>{const l=Math.hypot(...a);return [a[0]/l,a[1]/l,a[2]/l];};
const cross=(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]];

/** The harness body, its dated sea at `t`, and the sampler + classifier at that sea. */
export function harnessWorld(t=HARNESS_DATE){
  const sys=system(refByAddress(galaxies(HARNESS_SEED)[0],HARNESS_SYSTEM_ID));
  const body=[...sys.planets,...sys.moons].find(b=>b.id===HARNESS_BODY_ID);
  const star=sys.stars[0],systemAddress=parseAddress(sys.ref.id);
  const address=value=>({...systemAddress,slot:value.id.slice(sys.ref.id.length+1)});
  const starLc=starLifecycle(HARNESS_SEED,address(star),star),planetLc=planetLifecycle(HARNESS_SEED,address(body),body,starLc);
  const sea=planetSnapshotAt(HARNESS_SEED,body.id,body,planetLc,starLc,t,CURRENT_VERSIONS).ocean.renderWaterLevelM;
  return {body,sea,sample:terrainSampler(body,undefined,undefined,undefined,sea),classify:biomeClassifier(body,sea)};
}

/** Tangent-plane offset (metres east/north) from a unit direction. */
export function offsetDirection(world,centre,eastM,northM){
  const ref=Math.abs(centre[1])>.92?[1,0,0]:[0,1,0],e=norm(cross(ref,centre)),n=cross(centre,e),k=1/world.body.radiusM;
  return norm([0,1,2].map(i=>centre[i]+k*(eastM*e[i]+northM*n[i])));
}

/** Evaluates SITE_RULE at `direction`; returns the measured values and `pass`. */
export function evaluateSite(world,direction,rule=SITE_RULE){
  const at=d=>{const h=world.sample(d).groundHeightM;return {h,c:world.classify(d,h,1)};};
  const centre=at(direction);
  const out={heightM:centre.h,aboveSeaM:centre.h-world.sea,biome:centre.c.name,forestPotential:centre.c.forestPotential,
    effectiveMoisture:centre.c.effectiveMoisture,forestSuitability01:centre.c.forestSuitability01,ringForestShare:0,minRingAboveSeaM:Infinity,minRingMoisture:Infinity,pass:false};
  const centreOk=centre.c.forestPotential&&out.aboveSeaM>=rule.minAboveSeaM&&out.effectiveMoisture>=rule.minMoisture;
  let forest=0,n=0;
  for(const R of rule.ringsM)for(let i=0;i<rule.ringSamples;i++){
    const a=2*Math.PI*i/rule.ringSamples,s=at(offsetDirection(world,direction,R*Math.cos(a),R*Math.sin(a)));
    n++;if(s.c.forestPotential)forest++;
    out.minRingAboveSeaM=Math.min(out.minRingAboveSeaM,s.h-world.sea);out.minRingMoisture=Math.min(out.minRingMoisture,s.c.effectiveMoisture);
  }
  out.ringForestShare=forest/n;
  out.pass=centreOk&&out.ringForestShare>=rule.minRingForestShare&&out.minRingAboveSeaM>=rule.minAboveSeaM;
  return out;
}

/** The nearest passing site on a sunflower disc around `origin` (index order
 * is radius order), or null. Pure function of terrain, classifier and rule. */
export function searchSite(world,origin=SITE_A_LEGACY,search=SITE_SEARCH,rule=SITE_RULE){
  const G=Math.PI*(3-Math.sqrt(5));
  for(let i=0;i<search.points;i++){
    const r=search.radiusM*Math.sqrt((i+.5)/search.points),a=i*G;
    const direction=offsetDirection(world,origin,r*Math.cos(a),r*Math.sin(a));
    const site=evaluateSite(world,direction,rule);
    if(site.pass)return {index:i,direction,distanceM:world.body.radiusM*Math.acos(Math.min(1,direction.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*origin[k],0))),...site};
  }
  return null;
}

958: function ihash(x: number, y: number, z: number, s: number) {
959:   let h =
960:     Math.imul(x, 0x9e3779b1) ^
961:     Math.imul(y, 0x85ebca77) ^
962:     Math.imul(z, 0xc2b2ae3d) ^
963:     Math.imul(s, 0x27d4eb2f);
964:   h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 16), 0x85ebca6b);
965:   h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 0xc2b2ae35);
966:   h ^= h >>> 16;
967:   return (h >>> 0) / 4294967295;
968: }
969: export function noise(x: number, y: number, z: number, s: number) {
970:   const ix = Math.floor(x),
971:     iy = Math.floor(y),
972:     iz = Math.floor(z);
973:   let a = x - ix,
974:     b = y - iy,
975:     c = z - iz;
976:   a = a * a * (3 - 2 * a);
977:   b = b * b * (3 - 2 * b);
978:   c = c * c * (3 - 2 * c);
979:   let value = 0;
980:   for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++)
981:     for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++)
982:       for (let dz = 0; dz < 2; dz++)
983:         value +=
984:           ihash(ix + dx, iy + dy, iz + dz, s) *
985:           (dx ? a : 1 - a) *
986:           (dy ? b : 1 - b) *
987:           (dz ? c : 1 - c);
988:   return value;
989: }

35: /** Evaluates SITE_RULE at `direction`; returns the measured values and `pass`. */
36: export function evaluateSite(world,direction,rule=SITE_RULE){
37:   const at=d=>{const h=world.sample(d).groundHeightM;return {h,c:world.classify(d,h,1)};};
38:   const centre=at(direction);
39:   const out={heightM:centre.h,aboveSeaM:centre.h-world.sea,biome:centre.c.name,forestPotential:centre.c.forestPotential,
40:     effectiveMoisture:centre.c.effectiveMoisture,forestSuitability01:centre.c.forestSuitability01,ringForestShare:0,minRingAboveSeaM:Infinity,minRingMoisture:Infinity,pass:false};
41:   const centreOk=centre.c.forestPotential&&out.aboveSeaM>=rule.minAboveSeaM&&out.effectiveMoisture>=rule.minMoisture;
42:   let forest=0,n=0;
43:   for(const R of rule.ringsM)for(let i=0;i<rule.ringSamples;i++){
44:     const a=2*Math.PI*i/rule.ringSamples,s=at(offsetDirection(world,direction,R*Math.cos(a),R*Math.sin(a)));
45:     n++;if(s.c.forestPotential)forest++;
46:     out.minRingAboveSeaM=Math.min(out.minRingAboveSeaM,s.h-world.sea);out.minRingMoisture=Math.min(out.minRingMoisture,s.c.effectiveMoisture);
47:   }
48:   out.ringForestShare=forest/n;
49:   out.pass=centreOk&&out.ringForestShare>=rule.minRingForestShare&&out.minRingAboveSeaM>=rule.minAboveSeaM;
50:   return out;
51: }

VERDICT: APPROVE-WITH-FIXES

MUST-FIX

1. **Fell-colour acceptance was weakened beyond pin-only ownership.** `tests/terrain-climate-colour.test.mjs:112` clears `snowFraction` before adding samples to the visible-zone assertion at line 124. This tests hypothetical exposed substrate, not the rendered climate zone. The explanation is physically sound—Tw < 271 K implies no melting—but unchanged colour thresholds do not preserve the original assertion. **Fix:** hand this semantic change to the test owner; separate substrate-colour coverage from live climate coverage, explicitly asserting that these live columns are snow-covered.

2. **Out-of-scope file changed.** `tests/surface-biomes-climate.test.mjs:43` updates the classifier digest and climate version, but this file is absent from the supplied `files_owned` list. **Fix:** revert this change from the package and report the old/new pins to the coordinator, or record an explicit ownership amendment.

SHOULD

1. **Correct calibration provenance.** `docs/surface-climate-v2.md:37` describes both unsuccessful scans as using P_REF 1000. The coarse second scan actually varies P_REF from 400 through 1000 (`test-results/climate-m1/calibrate-zonal-2.mjs:66`). State that accurately; failure of finite candidate grids does not establish impossibility at 1000.

2. **Correct the SITE_A baseline label.** `docs/surface-climate-v2.md:98` calls the shipped site a pass under a rule requiring ≥1 K Tc margin while reporting 0.549 K. Separate the original SITE_RULE pass from the strengthened-rule failure.

3. **Add an explicit live half-range-floor case.** `tests/surface-climate.test.mjs:172` exercises a hot high-tilt world, but does not require the zero-floor branch to execute. Add a seasonless hot column whose annual mean exceeds the saturated ceiling; assert half=0, warmest=coldest=annual, finite PET/PDD, and zero forest.

Notes

- No formula or indexing defect found: H/G endpoints and seasonless PDD (`src/surface-climate.ts:157–182`), warm-column lapse and symmetric ceiling (`:423–431`), PET/P/AI and heat suppression (`:445–469`) match the documented model. Both latitude interpolators correctly reach the last row at x=1 (`:417–421`, `:436–440`). `dryBelt01` reads the unweighted subsidence Gaussian.
- Static inspection found no banned platform operations or per-sample allocations, including the called noise implementation. Tables use deterministic math outside sampling. Timing remains unverified.
- The Earth ELA negative (`tests/climate-earth-reference.test.mjs:40`) reconstructs the shipped annual-mean rule meaningfully. The polar-ITCZ negative (`tests/acceptance/climate-agreement.test.mjs:100`) compares the shipped fixed belt over the same selected corpus; coverage counts prevent vacuous success.
- **P_REF 650 is defensible as the documented calibration decision:** the scan uses Earth reference targets, not SITE_A or catalogue outcomes. This is calibration against prescribed acceptance targets; I found no evidence of site-preserving retuning.
- **SITE_A STOP is sound.** The scan reproduces the search directions and checks necessary centre clauses (`test-results/climate-m1/site-disc.mjs:19`). With zero moisture-qualified centres, ring evaluation cannot produce a passing replacement. Keep the STOP explicit; this verdict does not authorize proceeding past it.
